## Project Avalon: 32 overlapping investigation clusters {#avalon-clusters}

**John’s nine starting clusters lead this map.** Clusters 10–32 add care, clinical, research, information and process relationships from the inventory. Every topic A001–A336 appears in one or more clusters. Memberships are GPT / Codex’s editorial research routes, open to correction; each originating source retains its own type, date, qualifiers and status.

Open a cluster for its purpose, source entry points and topic links. Each topic link returns to the full question, investigation reason and forum sources in the [336-topic inventory](#avalon-scope-inventory). The [topic-to-cluster index](#avalon-cluster-topic-index) gives the reverse route. Cluster counts describe navigation, with overlapping membership allowed.

The first seven clusters foreground contributors, source forms and settings. Later clusters add mechanisms, care needs, methods, consequences and governing processes. A doctor can also be a patient; a case report can enter a hearing; a legal decision can affect access to care. [Topologetics]({{ '/TOPOLOGETICS/#health-scope-clusters' | relative_url }}) keeps these relationships recoverable through the [1888 intelligence-properties trajectory]({{ '/1888/' | relative_url }}), [10+1]({{ '/NETWORK/ten-plus-one/' | relative_url }}) and [Symmetric Grace]({{ '/NETWORK/asymmetric-grace/' | relative_url }}).

| Cluster | Inquiry purpose | Linked topics |
|---|---|---|
| [Cluster 1 — Doctors and other medical professionals: combined testimony](#avalon-cluster-01) | Bring together doctors, nurses, pharmacists, pathologists, allied professionals and clinicians who become patients. | 21 |
| [Cluster 2 — Court cases, legal claims and compensation proceedings](#avalon-cluster-02) | Follow access to a remedy from a complaint or filing through the actual proceedings, decisions, appeals and support. | 24 |
| [Cluster 3 — Hearings, inquiries and other government topics](#avalon-cluster-03) | Recover parliamentary records, public submissions, executive decisions, regulatory responsibilities and the work that follows. | 71 |
| [Cluster 4 — Peer-reviewed papers and other scientific publications](#avalon-cluster-04) | Connect journal papers, case reports, reviews, preprints, technical reports and methodological critiques with the questions they investigate. | 96 |
| [Cluster 5 — Funeral, embalming, crematorium and cemetery accounts](#avalon-cluster-05) | Bring death-care workers’ reports, capacity observations, material findings and cemetery observations into a recoverable field. | 15 |
| [Cluster 6 — Military operations, research programmes and service conditions](#avalon-cluster-06) | Examine military medical records, mandates, procurement, research programmes, command responsibilities and claims about operational involvement. | 18 |
| [Cluster 7 — Rare public debates and direct exchanges](#avalon-cluster-07) | Locate occasions where differing positions can address each other, including debates, cross-examination and substantive public replies. | 15 |
| [Cluster 8 — Unwarranted mandates and unchallenged emergency measures](#avalon-cluster-08) | Use John’s starting lens to examine warrant, necessity, proportionality, alternatives, exemptions, consequences and access to challenge in each measure. | 59 |
| [Cluster 9 — Weaponized mass psychology, framing and behavioural influence](#avalon-cluster-09) | Investigate how fear, shame, belonging, authority, repetition, targeting and visibility may shape interpretation, choice and participation. | 41 |
| [Cluster 10 — People’s own accounts and family testimony](#avalon-cluster-10) | Preserve lived timelines, help-seeking, uncertainties, personal priorities and the work of relatives or friends. | 116 |
| [Cluster 11 — Clinical presentations, diagnosis and reassessment](#avalon-cluster-11) | Keep specific clinical conditions and diagnostic pathways visible within the wider field. | 115 |
| [Cluster 12 — Treatment, rehabilitation and practical independence](#avalon-cluster-12) | Connect diagnostic understanding with accessible care, recovery, mobility and goals chosen by affected people. | 104 |
| [Cluster 13 — Deaths, mortality patterns, demography and service demand](#avalon-cluster-13) | Connect individual records and local observations with appropriately defined population comparisons. | 47 |
| [Cluster 14 — Pregnancy, fertility, infant feeding and reproductive health](#avalon-cluster-14) | Follow reproductive questions across maternal histories, conception, pregnancy, birth and later infant health. | 28 |
| [Cluster 15 — Children, development, education and family care](#avalon-cluster-15) | Connect childhood health, research participation, development, school access and continuing family responsibilities. | 42 |
| [Cluster 16 — Products, manufacturing, batches and administration](#avalon-cluster-16) | Preserve what was actually used, how it was made and handled, and how it was administered. | 43 |
| [Cluster 17 — Reporting systems, safety surveillance and continuing follow-up](#avalon-cluster-17) | Follow a concern from the affected person through recording, analysis, action and later contact. | 72 |
| [Cluster 18 — Privacy, data access, records and continuity](#avalon-cluster-18) | Make the relationship between information rights, research access and continuing memory inspectable. | 57 |
| [Cluster 19 — Commercial incentives, money, funding and allocation](#avalon-cluster-19) | Trace costs and financial relationships through research, messaging, product decisions, insurance and care. | 69 |
| [Cluster 20 — Media, censorship, platform algorithms and discoverability](#avalon-cluster-20) | Recover what becomes visible, hidden, reframed, circulated or corrected across communication channels. | 47 |
| [Cluster 21 — Laboratory methods, mechanisms and material identification](#avalon-cluster-21) | Connect laboratory observations with reproducible methods and the clinical questions they may inform. | 80 |
| [Cluster 22 — Study design, comparators, endpoints and causal models](#avalon-cluster-22) | Ask which observations would distinguish explanations and which question each design can answer. | 118 |
| [Cluster 23 — Institutional response, correction and accountable repair](#avalon-cluster-23) | Follow notice through investigation, response, changed conduct, continuing help and review. | 104 |
| [Cluster 24 — Community support, dignity, grief and cooperation](#avalon-cluster-24) | Preserve what helps people contribute, receive help, disagree, recover and remain connected. | 74 |
| [Cluster 25 — Work, military service, aviation, sport and return to activity](#avalon-cluster-25) | Connect clinical concerns with occupational demands, safety-critical tasks, performance and livelihood. | 39 |
| [Cluster 26 — Consent, bodily agency and dependent-care decisions](#avalon-cluster-26) | Recover the actual choices and conditions in which decisions are made for oneself or another person. | 78 |
| [Cluster 27 — Infection, immune function and interacting conditions](#avalon-cluster-27) | Connect infection history, immune measurements, underlying conditions and subsequent health. | 70 |
| [Cluster 28 — Disability, household economics and meaningful life](#avalon-cluster-28) | Keep long-term function and the person’s priorities inside the assessment of consequences. | 113 |
| [Cluster 29 — Research freedom, publication, archives and collective review](#avalon-cluster-29) | Follow observations through study design, funding, submission, review, publication, access and later correction. | 70 |
| [Cluster 30 — A.I. assistance, source recovery and navigable research](#avalon-cluster-30) | Make A.I. useful in recovering, comparing and connecting material while preserving the originating object. | 64 |
| [Cluster 31 — Veterinary health, environment, biosafety and food systems](#avalon-cluster-31) | Keep species, environmental conditions and production systems inside connected health inquiry. | 32 |
| [Cluster 32 — Process quality, healthy ambiguity and the 1888 property field](#avalon-cluster-32) | Keep the process itself corrigible as the scope moves across people, evidence, institutions and consequences. | 137 |

### Open a cluster and follow its relationships {#avalon-cluster-details}

<details id="avalon-cluster-01" markdown="1">
<summary><strong>Cluster 1 — Doctors and other medical professionals: combined testimony</strong> · 21 linked topics</summary>

**Inquiry purpose:** Bring together doctors, nurses, pharmacists, pathologists, allied professionals and clinicians who become patients.

**Relations to preserve:** Keep role, first-hand experience, clinical records, professional interpretation and later follow-up distinguishable.

These routes combine named professional accounts, interview descriptions, testimony and an anonymous medical account. Each retains its own source form.

**Selected archive routes:** [#2629 — Charles Hoffe clinical-observation account](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?113668-Vaccination-Injuries-On-Record-for-Covid19/page132#post1587303); [#2676 — Gail Macrae nursing account and interview description](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?113668-Vaccination-Injuries-On-Record-for-Covid19/page134#post1591391); [#2810 — Masayasu Inoue statement reproduced in the archive](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?113668-Vaccination-Injuries-On-Record-for-Covid19/page141#post1609039); [#2948 — Anonymous military-doctor account reproduced by a reporter](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?113668-Vaccination-Injuries-On-Record-for-Covid19/page148#post1631304); [#2953 — Sabine Hazan research and testimony description](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?113668-Vaccination-Injuries-On-Record-for-Covid19/page148#post1632265); [#3117 — Senate-hearing description including Peter McCullough](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?113668-Vaccination-Injuries-On-Record-for-Covid19/page156#post1674797); [#3133 — Robert Sullivan’s professional and patient testimony description](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?113668-Vaccination-Injuries-On-Record-for-Covid19/page157#post1678507); [#3171 — Tina Peers clinical-interview description](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?113668-Vaccination-Injuries-On-Record-for-Covid19/page159#post1688609).

<details id="avalon-cluster-04" markdown="1">
<summary><strong>Cluster 4 — Peer-reviewed papers and other scientific publications</strong> · 96 linked topics</summary>

**Inquiry purpose:** Connect journal papers, case reports, reviews, preprints, technical reports and methodological critiques with the questions they investigate.

**Relations to preserve:** Record the publication type, version, population, methods, endpoints and author conclusions; recover review or retraction history where relevant.

Publication leads below retain the type and version described in the archive. Recover the originating record when checking peer review, revisions, methods or conclusions.

**Selected archive routes:** [#2019 — Journal reanalysis described and linked](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?113668-Vaccination-Injuries-On-Record-for-Covid19/page101#post1535936); [#2369 — Reproduced pathology case-report text](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?113668-Vaccination-Injuries-On-Record-for-Covid19/page119#post1552194); [#2505 — Autopsy-review preprint description](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?113668-Vaccination-Injuries-On-Record-for-Covid19/page126#post1568226); [#2518 — Adult cardiac-surveillance study description](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?113668-Vaccination-Injuries-On-Record-for-Covid19/page126#post1569074); [#2868 — Semen-quality cohort-study description](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?113668-Vaccination-Injuries-On-Record-for-Covid19/page144#post1618336); [#3146 — Italian cancer-hospitalization cohort description](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?113668-Vaccination-Injuries-On-Record-for-Covid19/page158#post1684018); [#3179 — Korean cancer-incidence study description](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?113668-Vaccination-Injuries-On-Record-for-Covid19/page159#post1691268); [#3193 — Description of three postmortem-material research preprints](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?113668-Vaccination-Injuries-On-Record-for-Covid19/page160#post1707922).

<details id="avalon-cluster-05" markdown="1">
<summary><strong>Cluster 5 — Funeral, embalming, crematorium and cemetery accounts</strong> · 15 linked topics</summary>

**Inquiry purpose:** Bring death-care workers’ reports, capacity observations, material findings and cemetery observations into a recoverable field.

**Relations to preserve:** Distinguish a worker’s account, a relative’s report, a visitor’s observation, service records, tissue analysis and population inference.

The celebrant, funeral-director and embalmer accounts are separated from the cemetery visitor’s observation. Their observations and subsequent research remain connected.

<details id="avalon-cluster-07" markdown="1">
<summary><strong>Cluster 7 — Rare public debates and direct exchanges</strong> · 15 linked topics</summary>

**Inquiry purpose:** Locate occasions where differing positions can address each other, including debates, cross-examination and substantive public replies.

**Relations to preserve:** Preserve the actual proposition, question, answer, reply opportunity, format and unresolved difference.

John’s “rare public debates” lens brings together formal debates, direct interviews and hearing exchanges while keeping their formats visible.

<details id="avalon-cluster-09" markdown="1">
<summary><strong>Cluster 9 — Weaponized mass psychology, framing and behavioural influence</strong> · 41 linked topics</summary>

**Inquiry purpose:** Investigate how fear, shame, belonging, authority, repetition, targeting and visibility may shape interpretation, choice and participation.

**Relations to preserve:** Follow particular messages, commissioning, distribution, audience conditions and consequences; apply scrutiny across institutions, media, communities and A.I. systems.

Constructive dialogue provides a useful comparison: the same attention to trust, familiarity and framing can be examined for whether it opens inquiry, narrows it or alters choice.

**Selected archive routes:** [#2746 — John’s account of a communication bridge](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?113668-Vaccination-Injuries-On-Record-for-Covid19/page138#post1599936); [#3002 — Public-health campaign report account](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?113668-Vaccination-Injuries-On-Record-for-Covid19/page151#post1640419); [#3016 — Reproduced campaign analysis](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?113668-Vaccination-Injuries-On-Record-for-Covid19/page151#post1643125); [#3052 — Inquiry question-design and witness-role account](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?113668-Vaccination-Injuries-On-Record-for-Covid19/page153#post1655749); [#3084 — Sharyl Attkisson media interview lead](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?113668-Vaccination-Injuries-On-Record-for-Covid19/page155#post1661236).

<details id="avalon-cluster-10" markdown="1">
<summary><strong>Cluster 10 — People’s own accounts and family testimony</strong> · 116 linked topics</summary>

**Inquiry purpose:** Preserve lived timelines, help-seeking, uncertainties, personal priorities and the work of relatives or friends.

**Relations to preserve:** Keep the person’s report, an attributed clinician’s statement and an editor’s interpretation separately recoverable.

<details id="avalon-cluster-11" markdown="1">
<summary><strong>Cluster 11 — Clinical presentations, diagnosis and reassessment</strong> · 115 linked topics</summary>

**Inquiry purpose:** Keep specific clinical conditions and diagnostic pathways visible within the wider field.

**Relations to preserve:** Preserve onset, prior health, tests, differential explanations, severity, function and reassessment.

<summary><strong>Cluster 12 — Treatment, rehabilitation and practical independence</strong> · 104 linked topics</summary>

**Inquiry purpose:** Connect diagnostic understanding with accessible care, recovery, mobility and goals chosen by affected people.

**Relations to preserve:** Keep the proposed intervention, indication, experience, response, adverse effects and follow-up together.

<details id="avalon-cluster-18" markdown="1">
<summary><strong>Cluster 18 — Privacy, data access, records and continuity</strong> · 57 linked topics</summary>

**Inquiry purpose:** Make the relationship between information rights, research access and continuing memory inspectable.

**Relations to preserve:** Keep permissions, versions, provenance, linkage, withdrawal, missing fields and repair routes attached.

The public moderation notes are sufficient to study audience changes; the linked private discussion was not accessed.

<details id="avalon-cluster-29" markdown="1">
<summary><strong>Cluster 29 — Research freedom, publication, archives and collective review</strong> · 70 linked topics</summary>

**Inquiry purpose:** Follow observations through study design, funding, submission, review, publication, access and later correction.

**Relations to preserve:** Retain versions, source locations, methods, conflicts, editorial decisions, authorship and opportunities for replication.

**Selected archive routes:** [#2372 — Archive starting point #2372](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?113668-Vaccination-Injuries-On-Record-for-Covid19/page119#post1552322); [#2375 — Archive starting point #2375](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?113668-Vaccination-Injuries-On-Record-for-Covid19/page119#post1552359); [#2505 — Autopsy-review preprint description](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?113668-Vaccination-Injuries-On-Record-for-Covid19/page126#post1568226); [#2522 — Archive starting point #2522](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?113668-Vaccination-Injuries-On-Record-for-Covid19/page127#post1569247); [#2778 — Archive starting point #2778](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?113668-Vaccination-Injuries-On-Record-for-Covid19/page139#post1602832); [#3029 — Archive starting point #3029](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?113668-Vaccination-Injuries-On-Record-for-Covid19/page152#post1645943).

<details id="avalon-cluster-30" markdown="1">
<summary><strong>Cluster 30 — A.I. assistance, source recovery and navigable research</strong> · 64 linked topics</summary>

**Inquiry purpose:** Make A.I. useful in recovering, comparing and connecting material while preserving the originating object.

**Relations to preserve:** Keep source versus generated text, access limits, qualifiers, privacy, corrections and the next useful action visible.

A.I.-generated venue identifications and outcome summaries are source suggestions. The next useful operation is recovery of the original recording, minutes and decision.

**Selected archive routes:** [#2057 — Archive starting point #2057](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?113668-Vaccination-Injuries-On-Record-for-Covid19/page103#post1537295); [#2132 — Archive starting point #2132](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?113668-Vaccination-Injuries-On-Record-for-Covid19/page107#post1539074); [#3197 — Participant asks where the clip originated](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?113668-Vaccination-Injuries-On-Record-for-Covid19/page160#post1708756); [#3198 — A.I.-assisted venue and date suggestion](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?113668-Vaccination-Injuries-On-Record-for-Covid19/page160#post1708768); [#3199 — Participant asks about the petition’s outcome](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?113668-Vaccination-Injuries-On-Record-for-Covid19/page160#post1708794); [#3200 — A.I.-assisted outcome summary and source suggestions](https://projectavalon.net/forum4/showthread.php?113668-Vaccination-Injuries-On-Record-for-Covid19/page160#post1708798).

<details id="avalon-cluster-31" markdown="1">
<summary><strong>Cluster 31 — Veterinary health, environment, biosafety and food systems</strong> · 32 linked topics</summary>

**Inquiry purpose:** Keep species, environmental conditions and production systems inside connected health inquiry.

**Relations to preserve:** Retain species, product, purpose, dose, exposure, monitoring, care, oversight and routes into human decisions.

### Follow a relation across clusters {#avalon-cluster-paths}

These are suggested research routes through existing topics. Each step retains its own source and open question.

| Starting object | Relations that keep the field recoverable | Useful clusters |
|---|---|---|
| A clinician’s concern | Professional account → clinical record → reporting entry → investigation → response and care | [1](#avalon-cluster-01), [11](#avalon-cluster-11), [17](#avalon-cluster-17), [23](#avalon-cluster-23), [12](#avalon-cluster-12) |
| A death-care observation | Worker’s role and observation → sample and method → clinical history → population comparison | [5](#avalon-cluster-05), [21](#avalon-cluster-21), [11](#avalon-cluster-11), [13](#avalon-cluster-13), [22](#avalon-cluster-22) |
| A mandate or emergency measure | Authority and purpose → evidence and alternatives → affected people → consequences → challenge and repair | [8](#avalon-cluster-08), [3](#avalon-cluster-03), [22](#avalon-cluster-22), [26](#avalon-cluster-26), [28](#avalon-cluster-28), [2](#avalon-cluster-02) |
| A mass-psychology hypothesis | Message and commissioning → distribution and classification → audience conditions → interpretation and choice → notice and later conduct | [9](#avalon-cluster-09), [19](#avalon-cluster-19), [20](#avalon-cluster-20), [24](#avalon-cluster-24), [30](#avalon-cluster-30), [32](#avalon-cluster-32) |
| A person’s care needs | Lived account → diagnostic and rehabilitation pathway → household support → agency and meaningful participation | [10](#avalon-cluster-10), [11](#avalon-cluster-11), [12](#avalon-cluster-12), [28](#avalon-cluster-28), [26](#avalon-cluster-26) |

A cluster membership is revisable. A useful correction identifies the topic, the relation that is missing or misplaced, the source and what changes in the next inquiry. The evaluator’s framing remains available for the same scrutiny as the institutions, publications, communities and participants being examined.
